# application/

This directory contains the FastAPI application entry point and the Gradio chat UI.

| File             | Purpose                                                          |
|-----------------|------------------------------------------------------------------|
| `main.py`        | FastAPI app — all endpoints (chat, feedback, encrypted variants) |
| `run_ui.py`      | Gradio chat UI (connects to the API via HTTP)                    |
| `Launcher.py`    | CLI launcher — starts API, UI, or both                           |
| `fonts/`         | Optional custom font files for the Gradio UI                     |

## Usage

```bash
# Start API server only
python application/Launcher.py api

# Start Gradio UI only
python application/Launcher.py ui

# Start both
python application/Launcher.py all
```

## Environment

The app reads `API_BASE_URL` env var to know where the API is running (for the UI):
```
API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:8000
```



####################################################################################################
FILE: application\launcher.py
####################################################################################################

"""
application/Launcher.py
=======================
Unified launcher for the YONO Retail Chatbot.

Modes
-----
api     Starts the FastAPI backend only.
ui      Starts the HTML UI only (stdlib HTTP server).
all     Starts both API and UI in separate processes.

Usage
-----
    python application/Launcher.py api
    python application/Launcher.py ui
    python application/Launcher.py all

Environment variables
---------------------
APP_PORT
    FastAPI port. Defaults to 8000. Auto-increments if the port is busy.
UI_PORT
    UI port. Defaults to 7860. Auto-increments if the port is busy.
BACKEND_URL
    Override the backend URL seen by the UI. Derived from APP_PORT by default.
"""

import argparse
import logging
import multiprocessing
import os
import socket
import sys

# Ensure the project root is on sys.path for this process before any local imports.
_PROJECT_ROOT: str = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
if _PROJECT_ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _PROJECT_ROOT)

from dotenv import load_dotenv  # noqa: E402 — must follow path fix

load_dotenv()

# Capture the parent's full sys.path so child processes can reproduce it on Windows spawn.
_PARENT_SYSPATH: list = list(sys.path)

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("yb_prelogin_chat")


# Module-level helper functions.
# These must remain at module level because multiprocessing on Windows uses the
# spawn start method and requires picklable targets.

def _find_free_port(start: int, retries: int = 20) -> int:
    """
    Return the first TCP port >= start that is not currently in use.

    Parameters
    ----------
    start : int
        Port number to begin scanning from.
    retries : int
        Maximum number of ports to check before raising.

    Returns
    -------
    int
        A free port number.

    Raises
    ------
    RuntimeError
        When no free port is found within the retry range.
    """
    for port in range(start, start + retries):
        with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
            sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
            try:
                sock.bind(("0.0.0.0", port))
                return port
            except OSError:
                continue
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Could not find a free port in range {start} to {start + retries - 1}."
    )


def _path_initializer(extra_paths: list) -> None:
    """
    Restore the parent process sys.path inside a child process.

    On Windows, multiprocessing uses the spawn start method. Each child starts
    a fresh Python interpreter that only knows the default sys.path. Any paths
    added by the parent (project root, virtualenv extras) are invisible unless
    re-injected here before any import happens.

    Parameters
    ----------
    extra_paths : list
        The parent's full sys.path snapshot captured in _PARENT_SYSPATH.
    """
    for path in reversed(extra_paths):
        if path and path not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, path)
    # Re-apply .env in the child process so environment variables are available.
    from dotenv import load_dotenv as _ld
    _ld()


def _child_api(extra_paths: list, port: int) -> None:
    """
    Child-process entry point: restore sys.path then start the API server.

    Parameters
    ----------
    extra_paths : list
        Parent sys.path snapshot passed by ChatbotLauncher.start_all.
    port : int
        TCP port for the API server to bind to.
    """
    _path_initializer(extra_paths)
    import uvicorn
    from config import get_app_config

    cfg = get_app_config()
    LOGGER.info("API listening on http://%s:%d", cfg.host, port)
    uvicorn.run(
        "application.main:app",
        host=cfg.host,
        port=port,
        workers=1,
        reload=(cfg.env == "development"),
        log_level=cfg.log_level.lower(),
    )


def _child_ui(extra_paths: list, port: int, backend_url: str) -> None:
    """
    Child-process entry point: restore sys.path then start the UI server.

    Parameters
    ----------
    extra_paths : list
        Parent sys.path snapshot passed by ChatbotLauncher.start_all.
    port : int
        TCP port for the UI server to bind to.
    backend_url : str
        Backend API URL injected into the UI via environment variable.
    """
    _path_initializer(extra_paths)
    os.environ["UI_PORT"] = str(port)
    os.environ["BACKEND_URL"] = backend_url
    from application.run_ui import main as ui_main
    ui_main()


class ChatbotLauncher:
    """
    Manages launching the YONO Retail Chatbot API, UI, or both.

    This class coordinates port allocation and process management so that
    either component can be started in isolation or together.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> launcher = ChatbotLauncher()
    >>> launcher.start_api(port=8000)
    """

    def start_api(self, port: int) -> None:
        """
        Start the FastAPI backend with uvicorn on the given port.

        Parameters
        ----------
        port : int
            TCP port number for the API server.
        """
        import uvicorn
        from config import get_app_config

        cfg = get_app_config()
        LOGGER.info("Starting API server on http://%s:%d", cfg.host, port)
        uvicorn.run(
            "application.main:app",
            host=cfg.host,
            port=port,
            workers=1,
            reload=(cfg.env == "development"),
            log_level=cfg.log_level.lower(),
        )

    def start_ui(self, port: int, backend_url: str) -> None:
        """
        Start the HTML chat UI using the stdlib HTTP server.

        Parameters
        ----------
        port : int
            TCP port number for the UI server.
        backend_url : str
            Backend API URL made available to the UI via environment variable.
        """
        os.environ["UI_PORT"] = str(port)
        os.environ["BACKEND_URL"] = backend_url
        from application.run_ui import main as ui_main
        LOGGER.info("Starting HTML UI on port %d (backend: %s)", port, backend_url)
        ui_main()

    def start_all(self, api_port: int, ui_port: int, backend_url: str) -> None:
        """
        Start both the API and UI as separate daemon processes.

        Blocks until both processes exit or a KeyboardInterrupt is received.
        On interrupt, both processes are gracefully terminated.

        Parameters
        ----------
        api_port : int
            TCP port for the API server.
        ui_port : int
            TCP port for the UI server.
        backend_url : str
            Backend API URL passed to the UI process.
        """
        LOGGER.info("Starting API + UI.")
        LOGGER.info("API  -> http://0.0.0.0:%d", api_port)
        LOGGER.info("UI   -> http://127.0.0.1:%d  (backend: %s)", ui_port, backend_url)

        api_proc = multiprocessing.Process(
            target=_child_api,
            args=(_PARENT_SYSPATH, api_port),
            daemon=True,
            name="yono-api",
        )
        ui_proc = multiprocessing.Process(
            target=_child_ui,
            args=(_PARENT_SYSPATH, ui_port, backend_url),
            daemon=True,
            name="yono-ui",
        )

        api_proc.start()
        ui_proc.start()

        try:
            api_proc.join()
            ui_proc.join()
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            LOGGER.info("Interrupt received. Shutting down.")
            api_proc.terminate()
            ui_proc.terminate()
            api_proc.join(timeout=5)
            ui_proc.join(timeout=5)


def main() -> None:
    """
    Parse CLI arguments, resolve free ports, and launch the requested component.

    Notes
    -----
    This is the CLI entry point. It reads APP_PORT and UI_PORT from the
    environment, finds free ports starting from those values, then delegates
    to ChatbotLauncher.
    """
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="YONO Retail Chatbot Launcher",
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
        epilog=(
            "Examples:\n"
            "  python application/Launcher.py api      # API only (default port 8000)\n"
            "  python application/Launcher.py ui       # UI only  (default port 7860)\n"
            "  python application/Launcher.py all      # API + UI together\n"
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "mode",
        choices=["api", "ui", "all"],
        help="Component to launch.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    api_port_want = int(os.environ.get("APP_PORT", "8000"))
    ui_port_want = int(os.environ.get("UI_PORT", "7860"))

    launcher = ChatbotLauncher()

    if args.mode == "api":
        api_port = _find_free_port(api_port_want)
        if api_port != api_port_want:
            LOGGER.info("Port %d busy — using %d instead.", api_port_want, api_port)
        LOGGER.info("Starting API server on port %d.", api_port)
        launcher.start_api(api_port)
        return

    if args.mode == "ui":
        ui_port = _find_free_port(ui_port_want)
        if ui_port != ui_port_want:
            LOGGER.info("Port %d busy — using %d instead.", ui_port_want, ui_port)
        backend_url = os.environ.get("BACKEND_URL", f"http://127.0.0.1:{api_port_want}")
        LOGGER.info("Starting HTML UI on port %d.", ui_port)
        launcher.start_ui(ui_port, backend_url)
        return

    # Launch both API and UI together.
    api_port = _find_free_port(api_port_want)
    ui_port = _find_free_port(ui_port_want)
    backend_url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{api_port}"

    if api_port != api_port_want:
        LOGGER.info("Port %d busy — API will use %d.", api_port_want, api_port)
    if ui_port != ui_port_want:
        LOGGER.info("Port %d busy — UI will use %d.", ui_port_want, ui_port)

    launcher.start_all(api_port, ui_port, backend_url)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # freeze_support is required on Windows for frozen (PyInstaller) executables.
    multiprocessing.freeze_support()
    main()



####################################################################################################
FILE: application\main.py
####################################################################################################

"""
application/main.py
===================
FastAPI application entry point for the YONO Retail Chatbot.

Endpoints
---------
GET  /health                  Liveness probe.
GET  /test                    Smoke test.
POST /yb_chatapi_noenc        Plain-text chat (no encryption).
POST /yb_chatapi_enc          AES-GCM encrypted chat (when encryption is available).
POST /yb_feedback_noenc       Plain-text feedback submission.
POST /yb_feedback_enc         Encrypted feedback submission (when encryption is available).

Architecture
------------
All business logic lives in genai/agents/pipeline.py.
Conversation storage is handled inside the agent nodes.
The encrypted endpoints use RSA-OAEP key exchange plus AES-GCM body encryption.
FastAPI and uvicorn with configurable workers handle concurrent sessions through asyncio.
All I/O is non-blocking.
"""

import json
import logging
import time
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from datetime import datetime
from typing import AsyncGenerator

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from config import get_app_config
from genai.agents.pipeline import agent_backend
from genai.agents.models import ChatRequest, FeedbackRequest
from genai.core.conversation_db import ConversationDB
from utils.logger import logger

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("yb_prelogin_chat")

# Encrypted request/response schemas and utilities are preserved from the
# existing secure_utilities package. They are imported conditionally so that
# the application can still start when the package is unavailable.
try:
    from secure_utilities.enc_schemas import SecureRequest
    from secure_utilities.enc_utils import (
        decrypt_aes_key,
        decrypt_request,
        encrypt_request,
        sign_data,
        verify_signature,
    )
    from secure_utilities.enc_const import ERROR_CODE, ERROR_DESCRIPTION
    from secure_utilities.security import DecryptionFilter
    _ENCRYPTION_AVAILABLE: bool = True
except Exception as _enc_err:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
    LOGGER.warning(
        "secure_utilities unavailable (%s: %s) — encrypted endpoints will be disabled.",
        type(_enc_err).__name__, _enc_err,
    )
    _ENCRYPTION_AVAILABLE = False

_db = ConversationDB()
_app_cfg = get_app_config()


@asynccontextmanager
async def _lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncGenerator[None, None]:
    """
    Manage shared resource lifecycle for the FastAPI application.

    Parameters
    ----------
    app : FastAPI
        The FastAPI application instance.

    Yields
    ------
    None
        Control is yielded to the application after startup completes.
    """
    LOGGER.info("Starting YONO Retail Chatbot API v2.0.")
    await _db.init()
    LOGGER.info("Database initialised. API ready.")
    yield
    LOGGER.info("Shutting down.")
    await _db.close()

    # Close the Elasticsearch client if the vector store module is loaded.
    try:
        from genai.core.vector_store import close_es_client
        await close_es_client()
    except Exception:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
        pass


class YonoRetailApi:
    """
    Factory class that builds and configures the YONO Retail FastAPI application.

    This class encapsulates application creation so that the app instance can
    be produced and tested independently of module-level side effects.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> api = YonoRetailApi()
    >>> application = api.create_app()
    """

    def create_app(self) -> FastAPI:
        """
        Build and return a fully configured FastAPI application instance.

        Returns
        -------
        FastAPI
            Application with all middleware and routes registered.
        """
        application = FastAPI(
            title="YONO Retail Chatbot API",
            version="2.0.0",
            description="Enterprise RAG chatbot for YONO Retail Banking.",
            lifespan=_lifespan,
        )

        application.add_middleware(
            CORSMiddleware,
            allow_origins=["*"],
            allow_credentials=True,
            allow_methods=["*"],
            allow_headers=["*"],
        )

        if _ENCRYPTION_AVAILABLE:
            application.add_middleware(DecryptionFilter)

        self._register_routes(application)
        return application

    def _register_routes(self, application: FastAPI) -> None:
        """
        Register all API routes on the given application.

        Parameters
        ----------
        application : FastAPI
            The FastAPI instance to register routes on.
        """
        application.add_api_route(
            "/health",
            _health_check,
            methods=["GET"],
            tags=["Ops"],
        )
        application.add_api_route(
            "/test",
            _smoke_test,
            methods=["GET"],
            tags=["Ops"],
        )
        application.add_api_route(
            "/yb_chatapi_noenc",
            _yb_chatapi_noenc,
            methods=["POST"],
            tags=["Chat"],
        )
        application.add_api_route(
            "/yb_feedback_noenc",
            _yb_feedback_noenc,
            methods=["POST"],
            tags=["Feedback"],
        )

        if _ENCRYPTION_AVAILABLE:
            application.add_api_route(
                "/yb_chatapi_enc",
                _yb_chatapi_enc,
                methods=["POST"],
                tags=["Chat"],
            )
            application.add_api_route(
                "/yb_feedback_enc",
                _yb_feedback_enc,
                methods=["POST"],
                tags=["Feedback"],
            )


# Health and smoke endpoints.

async def _health_check() -> dict:
    """
    Liveness probe that returns 200 when the API is running.

    Returns
    -------
    dict
        Status and current UTC timestamp.
    """
    return {"status": "ok", "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()}


def _smoke_test() -> dict:
    """
    Smoke test endpoint.

    Returns
    -------
    dict
        Static greeting message.
    """
    return {"Hello": "YONO Retail Chatbot"}


# Unencrypted chat endpoint.

async def _yb_chatapi_noenc(request: ChatRequest) -> dict:
    """
    Process a plain-text chat request.

    Parameters
    ----------
    request : ChatRequest
        JSON body containing question and thread_id.

    Returns
    -------
    dict
        Response with keys answer and question_id.

    Raises
    ------
    HTTPException
        Raised with status 500 on unexpected processing errors.
    """
    start = time.time()
    LOGGER.info(
        "yb_chatapi_noenc | session=%s | q=%s",
        request.thread_id, request.question[:80],
    )
    try:
        agent_response, question_id = await agent_backend(request.question, request.thread_id)
        elapsed = time.time() - start
        LOGGER.info("yb_chatapi_noenc completed in %.2fs", elapsed)
        return {"answer": agent_response, "question_id": question_id}
    except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
        LOGGER.exception("yb_chatapi_noenc error")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc)) from exc


# Unencrypted feedback endpoint.

async def _yb_feedback_noenc(request: FeedbackRequest) -> dict:
    """
    Submit feedback for a plain-text chat response.

    Parameters
    ----------
    request : FeedbackRequest
        JSON body containing question_id and feedback.

    Returns
    -------
    dict
        Response with key message indicating success or failure.

    Raises
    ------
    HTTPException
        Raised with status 500 on unexpected processing errors.
    """
    LOGGER.info(
        "yb_feedback_noenc | qid=%s feedback=%s",
        request.question_id, request.feedback,
    )
    try:
        updated = await _db.update_feedback(request.question_id, request.feedback)
        msg = "Feedback submitted successfully" if updated else "Error while submitting the feedback"
        return {"message": msg}
    except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
        LOGGER.exception("yb_feedback_noenc error")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc)) from exc


# Encrypted endpoints are defined only when encryption is available.
# The _ENCRYPTION_AVAILABLE guard prevents NameError on SecureRequest at import time.

if _ENCRYPTION_AVAILABLE:
    async def _yb_chatapi_enc(request: Request, body: "SecureRequest") -> dict:
        """
        Process an AES-GCM encrypted chat request.

        The DecryptionFilter middleware decrypts the request body and stores
        plain_data and aes_key on request.state before this handler runs.

        Parameters
        ----------
        request : Request
            Starlette request carrying decrypted state from middleware.
        body : SecureRequest
            Encrypted request envelope.

        Returns
        -------
        dict
            Encrypted response envelope containing DIGI_SIGN.

        Raises
        ------
        HTTPException
            Raised with status 401 when the request signature is invalid.
            Raised with status 500 on unexpected processing errors.
        """
        start = time.time()
        LOGGER.info("yb_chatapi_enc | ref=%s", body.REQUEST_REFERENCE_NUMBER)
        try:
            is_valid = getattr(request.state, "is_valid", False)
            if not is_valid:
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=401,
                    detail={
                        "REQUEST_REFERENCE_NUMBER": body.REQUEST_REFERENCE_NUMBER,
                        "ERROR_CODE": ERROR_CODE,
                        "ERROR_DESCRIPTION": ERROR_DESCRIPTION,
                    },
                )

            plain_data = getattr(request.state, "plain_data", {})
            question = plain_data["question"]
            thread_id = plain_data["thread_id"]

            agent_response, question_id = await agent_backend(question, thread_id)
            ai_response = {"answer": agent_response, "question_id": question_id}

            aes_key = getattr(request.state, "aes_key", None)
            encrypted_ai_response = encrypt_request(ai_response, aes_key)
            ai_response_str = json.dumps(ai_response, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
            digi_sign = sign_data(ai_response_str)

            elapsed = time.time() - start
            LOGGER.info("yb_chatapi_enc completed in %.2fs", elapsed)

            return {
                "RESPONSE": encrypted_ai_response,
                "REQUEST_REFERENCE_NUMBER": body.REQUEST_REFERENCE_NUMBER,
                "RESPONSE_DATE": datetime.now().strftime("%d-%m-%Y %H:%M:%S"),
                "DIGI_SIGN": digi_sign,
            }
        except HTTPException:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
            LOGGER.exception("yb_chatapi_enc error")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    async def _yb_feedback_enc(request: Request, body: "SecureRequest") -> dict:
        """
        Submit encrypted feedback for a chat response.

        Parameters
        ----------
        request : Request
            Starlette request carrying decrypted state from middleware.
        body : SecureRequest
            Encrypted request envelope.

        Returns
        -------
        dict
            Encrypted response envelope containing DIGI_SIGN.

        Raises
        ------
        HTTPException
            Raised with status 401 when the request signature is invalid.
            Raised with status 500 on unexpected processing errors.
        """
        start = time.time()
        LOGGER.info("yb_feedback_enc | ref=%s", body.REQUEST_REFERENCE_NUMBER)
        try:
            is_valid = getattr(request.state, "is_valid", False)
            if not is_valid:
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=401,
                    detail={
                        "REQUEST_REFERENCE_NUMBER": body.REQUEST_REFERENCE_NUMBER,
                        "ERROR_CODE": ERROR_CODE,
                        "ERROR_DESCRIPTION": ERROR_DESCRIPTION,
                    },
                )

            plain_data = getattr(request.state, "plain_data", {})
            question_id = plain_data["question_id"]
            feedback = plain_data["feedback"]

            updated = await _db.update_feedback(question_id, feedback)
            feedback_response = {
                "message": (
                    "Feedback submitted successfully" if updated
                    else "Error while submitting the feedback"
                )
            }

            aes_key = getattr(request.state, "aes_key", None)
            encrypted_response = encrypt_request(feedback_response, aes_key)
            resp_str = json.dumps(feedback_response, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
            digi_sign = sign_data(resp_str)

            elapsed = time.time() - start
            LOGGER.info("yb_feedback_enc completed in %.2fs", elapsed)

            return {
                "RESPONSE": encrypted_response,
                "REQUEST_REFERENCE_NUMBER": body.REQUEST_REFERENCE_NUMBER,
                "RESPONSE_DATE": datetime.now().strftime("%d-%m-%Y %H:%M:%S"),
                "DIGI_SIGN": digi_sign,
            }
        except HTTPException:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
            LOGGER.exception("yb_feedback_enc error")
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc)) from exc


# Module-level app instance consumed by uvicorn and the Launcher.
app: FastAPI = YonoRetailApi().create_app()

  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "id": "a6b7c8d9-e0f1-2345-0123-345678900005",
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "import httpx\n",
    "\n",
    "def embed_texts_sync(texts, model_id, base_url, api_key):\n",
    "    \"\"\"Synchronous embedding call for notebook use.\"\"\"\n",
    "    url = f\"{base_url.rstrip('/')}/embeddings\"\n",
    "    headers = {\n",
    "        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n",
    "        'Authorization': f'Bearer {api_key}',\n",
    "    }\n",
    "    with httpx.Client(verify=False, timeout=120) as client:\n",
    "        response = client.post(url, json={'model': model_id, 'input': texts}, headers=headers)\n",
    "    response.raise_for_status()\n",
    "    data = sorted(response.json()['data'], key=lambda x: x['index'])\n",
    "    return [item['embedding'] for item in data]\n",
    "\n",
    "# Test with the first record\n",
    "test_text = records[0].combined_text\n",
    "print(f'Embedding text:\\n  {test_text[:120]}...')\n",
    "\n",
    "try:\n",
    "    vectors = embed_texts_sync([test_text], EMB_MODEL_ID, VLLM_BASE_EMB_URL, VLLM_API_KEY)\n",
    "    print(f'\\n✅ Embedding successful!')\n",
    "    print(f'   Vector dims : {len(vectors[0])}')\n",
    "    print(f'   First 5 values: {vectors[0][:5]}')\nexcept Exception as e:\n",
    "    print(f'\\n❌ Embedding failed: {e}')\n",
    "    print('Check VLLM_BASE_EMB_URL, VLLM_API_KEY, and EMB_MODEL_ID in .env')"
   ]
  },
  {

####################################################################################################
FILE: application\run_ui.py
####################################################################################################

"""
application/run_ui.py
=====================
Self-contained HTML UI for the YONO Retail Chatbot.

Serves a single-page application via the Python standard-library
``http.server.HTTPServer`` -- no Gradio, no extra dependencies.

The server:
- Renders the chat UI from an embedded HTML string.
- Proxies ``POST /yb_chatapi_noenc``, ``POST /yb_feedback_noenc``,
  and ``GET /health`` transparently to the FastAPI backend.
- Serves any static fonts placed in ``application/fonts/``.
- Opens the browser automatically on startup.

Configuration (environment variables)
--------------------------------------
UI_PORT      : HTTP port for the UI server  (default 7860)
BACKEND_URL  : FastAPI backend base URL     (default http://127.0.0.1:8000)

Usage
-----
    python application/run_ui.py
    # or via Launcher.py  (python application/Launcher.py ui)
"""

import json
import logging
import os
import threading
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
import webbrowser
from dataclasses import dataclass
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()

LOGGER: logging.Logger = logging.getLogger("yono_ui")

_UI_PORT: int = int(os.getenv("UI_PORT", "7860"))
_BACKEND_URL: str = os.getenv("BACKEND_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8000").rstrip("/")
_FONTS_DIR: Path = Path(__file__).parent / "fonts"


@dataclass
class UIServerConfig:
    """Configuration for the UI HTTP server.

    Parameters
    ----------
    ui_port : int
        TCP port on which the UI server listens.
    backend_url : str
        Base URL of the FastAPI backend that the server proxies requests to.
    """

    ui_port: int = _UI_PORT
    backend_url: str = _BACKEND_URL


# Embedded single-page application HTML

HTML: str = r"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8"/>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0"/>
<title>YONO Retail Chatbot - Enterprise Banking AI</title>
<style>
  /* Urbanist web font (served locally from /fonts/) */

  }

<script>
/* Session management */
const STORAGE_KEY = 'yono_thread_id';

function getThreadId() {
  let tid = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
  if (!tid) { tid = crypto.randomUUID(); localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, tid); }
  return tid;
}

function newChat() {
  const tid = crypto.randomUUID();
  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, tid);
  document.getElementById('messages').innerHTML = '';
  setFeedbackState(null, null);
  appendWelcome();
}

/* Page navigation */
function showPage(name, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('page-' + name).classList.add('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');

  // Show the pipeline strip only on the chat page
  const strip = document.getElementById('pipeline-strip');
  strip.style.display = (name === 'chat') ? 'flex' : 'none';
}

/* Message rendering */
function appendWelcome() {
  appendBotBubble(
    'Hello! I am your YONO Retail AI assistant. ' +
    'Ask me anything about YONO banking products, services, accounts, loans, or transactions.'
  );
}

function appendUserBubble(text) {
  const box = document.getElementById('messages');
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'msg user';
  el.innerHTML =
    '<div class="avatar">You</div>' +
    '<div class="bubble">' + escHtml(text) + '</div>';
  box.appendChild(el);
  scrollBottom();
  return el;
}

function appendBotBubble(text) {
  const box = document.getElementById('messages');
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'msg bot';
  el.innerHTML =
    '<div class="avatar">YR</div>' +
    '<div class="bubble">' + escHtml(text) + '</div>';
  box.appendChild(el);
  scrollBottom();
  return el;
}

function appendTyping() {
  const box = document.getElementById('messages');
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'msg bot typing';
  el.id = 'typing-indicator';
  el.innerHTML =
    '<div class="avatar">YR</div>' +
    '<div class="bubble">Thinking</div>';
  box.appendChild(el);
  scrollBottom();
  return el;
}

function removeTyping() {
  const t = document.getElementById('typing-indicator');
  if (t) t.remove();
}

function appendFeedbackRow(questionId) {
  const box = document.getElementById('messages');
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'feedback-row';
  el.id = 'feedback-row';
  el.innerHTML =
    '<button class="fb-btn" id="fb-up"   onclick="submitFeedback(\'thumbs_up\')"   title="Helpful">+ Helpful</button>' +
    '<button class="fb-btn" id="fb-down" onclick="submitFeedback(\'thumbs_down\')" title="Not helpful">- Not helpful</button>' +
    '<span class="fb-status" id="fb-status"></span>';
  el.dataset.questionId = questionId;
  box.appendChild(el);
  scrollBottom();
}

function scrollBottom() {
  const box = document.getElementById('messages');
  box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
}

function escHtml(s) {
  return s.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;')
          .replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;');
}

/* Feedback state */
let _currentQuestionId = null;

function setFeedbackState(qid, activeBtn) {
  _currentQuestionId = qid;
}

function submitFeedback(value) {
  const row = document.getElementById('feedback-row');
  if (!row) return;
  const qid = row.dataset.questionId;
  if (!qid) return;

  const statusEl = document.getElementById('fb-status');
  statusEl.textContent = 'Sending\u2026';

  fetch('/yb_feedback_noenc', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ question_id: qid, feedback: value })
  })
  .then(r => r.json())
  .then(() => {
    statusEl.textContent = '\u2713 Thank you!';
    document.getElementById('fb-up')  .classList.toggle('active-up',   value === 'thumbs_up');
    document.getElementById('fb-down').classList.toggle('active-down', value === 'thumbs_down');
    document.getElementById('fb-up')  .disabled = true;
    document.getElementById('fb-down').disabled = true;
  })
  .catch(() => { statusEl.textContent = 'Failed to submit.'; });
}

/* Send message */
function handleKey(e) {
  if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); sendMessage(); }
}

function autoResize(el) {
  el.style.height = 'auto';
  el.style.height = Math.min(el.scrollHeight, 150) + 'px';
}

function sendMessage() {
  const input = document.getElementById('msg-input');
  const text = input.value.trim();
  if (!text) return;

  input.value = '';
  input.style.height = 'auto';
  document.getElementById('send-btn').disabled = true;

  // Remove the previous feedback row before sending the next message
  const oldFb = document.getElementById('feedback-row');
  if (oldFb) oldFb.remove();

  appendUserBubble(text);
  const typing = appendTyping();

  fetch('/yb_chatapi_noenc', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ question: text, thread_id: getThreadId() })
  })
  .then(r => {
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status);
    return r.json();
  })
  .then(data => {
    removeTyping();
    const answer = data.answer || 'Sorry, I could not generate a response. Please try again.';
    appendBotBubble(answer);
    if (data.question_id) appendFeedbackRow(data.question_id);
  })
  .catch(err => {
    removeTyping();
    appendBotBubble('Unable to reach the chatbot service. Please check that the API server is running.');
    console.error('Chat error:', err);
  })
  .finally(() => {
    document.getElementById('send-btn').disabled = false;
    document.getElementById('msg-input').focus();
  });
}

/* Health poll - checks backend connectivity every 30 seconds */
function checkBackend() {
  fetch('/health')
    .then(r => {
      document.getElementById('status-banner').classList.toggle('visible', !r.ok);
    })
    .catch(() => {
      document.getElementById('status-banner').classList.add('visible');
    });
}

/* Initialise the page on DOM ready */
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  appendWelcome();
  checkBackend();
  setInterval(checkBackend, 30000);
  document.getElementById('msg-input').focus();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
"""


# HTTP request handler

class UiServer(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    """HTTP request handler that serves the SPA and proxies API calls.

    Handles GET requests for the SPA, font files, and health probe,
    and POST requests for the chat and feedback API endpoints.
    """

    # Proxy helper

    def _proxy(self, method: str) -> None:
        """Forward a request to the FastAPI backend and relay the response.

        Parameters
        ----------
        method : str
            HTTP method to use for the upstream request (GET or POST).
        """
        target = f"{_BACKEND_URL}{self.path}"
        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""

        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url=target,
            data=body if body else None,
            method=method,
            headers={
                "Content-Type": self.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"),
                "Accept": "application/json",
            },
        )
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as resp:
                data = resp.read()
                self.send_response(resp.status)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(data)
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            data = exc.read()
            self.send_response(exc.code)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(data)
        except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
            payload = json.dumps({"detail": f"Backend unreachable: {exc}"}).encode()
            self.send_response(502)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(payload)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(payload)

    # GET handler

    def do_GET(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        """Handle GET requests.

        Routes health probes to the backend, serves font files from disk,
        and returns the embedded SPA HTML for all other paths.
        """
        # Health probe - proxy to backend
        if self.path == "/health":
            self._proxy("GET")
            return

        # Font files - serve .ttf, .woff2, and .woff with correct MIME types
        if self.path.startswith("/fonts/"):
            fname = self.path[len("/fonts/"):]
            font_path = _FONTS_DIR / fname
            if font_path.is_file():
                data = font_path.read_bytes()
                ext = fname.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower()
                ctype = {
                    "ttf":   "font/ttf",
                    "woff2": "font/woff2",
                    "woff":  "font/woff",
                    "otf":   "font/otf",
                }.get(ext, "application/octet-stream")
                self.send_response(200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
                self.send_header("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=31536000, immutable")
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(data)
            else:
                self.send_response(404)
                self.end_headers()
            return

        # All other paths - serve the SPA
        page = HTML.encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(page)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(page)

    # POST handler

    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        """Handle POST requests.

        Proxies chat and feedback API calls to the backend.
        Returns 404 for any other path.
        """
        if self.path in ("/yb_chatapi_noenc", "/yb_feedback_noenc"):
            self._proxy("POST")
        else:
            self.send_response(404)
            self.end_headers()

    # Suppress default access log output

    def log_message(self, format: str, *args: object) -> None:  # noqa: D102,A002
        """Suppress the default per-request log output from BaseHTTPRequestHandler.

        Parameters
        ----------
        format : str
            Log format string (ignored).
        *args : object
            Positional arguments for the format string (ignored).
        """
        pass


# Entry point

def main() -> None:
    """Start the UI HTTP server and open the browser.

    Reads configuration from module-level constants, starts the HTTPServer
    on all interfaces, logs startup information, opens the browser after a
    short delay, and runs until a KeyboardInterrupt is received.
    """
    server = HTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", _UI_PORT), UiServer)
    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{_UI_PORT}"

    LOGGER.info("YONO Retail Chatbot UI starting on %s", url)
    LOGGER.info("Backend URL: %s", _BACKEND_URL)

    print(f"YONO Retail Chatbot UI  ->  {url}")
    print(f"Backend                 ->  {_BACKEND_URL}")
    print("Press Ctrl+C to stop.\n")

    # Open the browser after a short delay so the server is ready to accept connections
    threading.Timer(1.0, lambda: webbrowser.open(url)).start()

    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nUI server stopped.")
    finally:
        server.server_close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: data\README.md

# data/

This directory contains the data ingestion pipeline for the YONO Retail Chatbot.

## Files

| File                     | Purpose                                                         |
|-------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------|
| `csv_loader.py`          | Parses `dataset.csv` — handles merged cells, forward-fill      |
| `es_index_manager.py`    | Creates / verifies / deletes the Elasticsearch index           |
| `ingest_yono_retail.py`  | End-to-end ingestion pipeline (CLI + Python API)               |

## Index Mapping

The Elasticsearch index uses the mapping from [`../artifect1.txt`](../artifect1.txt):
- **`vector`** field: `dense_vector`, dims=2048, `bbq_hnsw`, cosine similarity
- 1 shard, 1 replica
- All Q&A metadata fields for BM25 keyword retrieval

## Running Ingestion

### Quick start (CLI)
```bash
# From the project root:
python data/ingest_yono_retail.py
```

### Options
```bash
python data/ingest_yono_retail.py --help

python data/ingest_yono_retail.py --dry-run          # preview only
python data/ingest_yono_retail.py --skip-if-exists   # skip if already indexed
python data/ingest_yono_retail.py --batch 100        # ES bulk size
python data/ingest_yono_retail.py --health-only      # connection test only
```

### Jupyter notebook
Open [`../application/notebook.ipynb`](../application/notebook.ipynb) for an interactive walkthrough with:
- Connection test
- Dataset preview with category breakdown
- Single embedding test
- Full ingestion
- kNN semantic search verification
- BM25 keyword search verification

## Environment Variables Required

```
ES_URL=https://your-elasticsearch-host:9200
ES_API_KEY=your-api-key
ES_INDEX=yono_retail_chunks
VLLM_BASE_EMB_URL=https://your-vllm-host/v1
VLLM_API_KEY=your-vllm-api-key
EMB_MODEL_ID=your-embedding-model-id
```

## How the Embedding Works

Each Q&A row is embedded as a single `combined_text` string:

```
Category: ATM/Debit Card Service. Question: What is Virtual Debit Card? Answer: Virtual Debit Card known as Electronic Card or e-Card, is a Debit Card created for ecommerce transactions...
```

This pattern ensures the embedding captures all three dimensions of the Q&A:
the domain context (category), the intent (question), and the factual content (answer).



####################################################################################################
FILE: data\csv_loader.py
####################################################################################################

"""
data/csv_loader.py
==================
CSV loader for the YONO Retail Q&A dataset (dataset.csv).

The CSV uses Excel-style merged cells where the Sno and Category columns are
only populated on the first row of each group.  Subsequent rows in the same
group have empty Sno and Category cells.

This module:
1. Parses the raw CSV with forward-fill for Sno and Category.
2. Skips rows where both Question and Answer are empty.
3. Returns clean QARecord dataclass instances ready for embedding.
4. Builds a rich combined_text field that gets embedded.

Usage
-----
from data.csv_loader import CsvLoader
records = CsvLoader().load("dataset.csv")
"""

import csv
import logging
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("yb_prelogin_chat")


@dataclass
class QARecord:
    """
    A single Q&A record parsed from the YONO Retail dataset.

    Attributes
    ----------
    sno : int
        Sequential number from the CSV, forward-filled across category rows.
    category : str
        Product category, for example ATM/Debit Card Service or Car Loan.
    question : str
        Raw question text.
    answer : str
        Raw answer text.
    record_type : str
        Row type from the CSV, for example Main Category.
    row_index : int
        Original zero-based row index within the CSV after the header.
    combined_text : str
        Concatenated text field that will be embedded and indexed.
    """

    sno: int
    category: str
    question: str
    answer: str
    record_type: str
    row_index: int
    combined_text: str = field(default="", init=False)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """Build combined_text after dataclass construction."""
        self.combined_text = _build_combined_text(
            category=self.category,
            question=self.question,
            answer=self.answer,
        )


def _clean(value: Optional[str]) -> str:
    """
    Strip leading/trailing whitespace and collapse internal whitespace in a cell value.

    Parameters
    ----------
    value : str or None
        Raw CSV cell value.

    Returns
    -------
    str
        Cleaned string; empty string when value is None or blank.
    """
    if not value:
        return ""
    # Collapse embedded newlines and multiple spaces into a single space.
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", value.strip())


def _build_combined_text(category: str, question: str, answer: str) -> str:
    """
    Build the combined text string used for vector embedding.

    Follows the pattern:
        "Category: <category>. Question: <question> Answer: <answer>"

    Parameters
    ----------
    category : str
        Cleaned category name.
    question : str
        Cleaned question text.
    answer : str
        Cleaned answer text.

    Returns
    -------
    str
        Single combined string ready for embedding.
    """
    return f"Category: {category}. Question: {question} Answer: {answer}"


class CsvLoader:
    """
    Loads and parses the YONO Retail Q&A CSV into QARecord instances.

    The CSV uses an Excel merged-cell convention where Sno and Category are
    only present on the first row of each logical group.  This class
    forward-fills both columns so every record has a valid value.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> loader = CsvLoader()
    >>> records = loader.load("dataset.csv")
    >>> print(len(records))
    """

    def load(self, path: "str | Path") -> list[QARecord]:
        """
        Load and parse the YONO Retail Q&A CSV.

        Parameters
        ----------
        path : str or Path
            Path to the dataset CSV file, for example dataset.csv.

        Returns
        -------
        list[QARecord]
            All non-empty Q&A records in row order.

        Raises
        ------
        FileNotFoundError
            When the file at path does not exist.
        ValueError
            When the CSV has no rows or is missing expected columns.
        """
        csv_path = Path(path)
        if not csv_path.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Dataset not found: {csv_path}")

        records: list[QARecord] = []

        # State carried forward across rows for merged-cell groups.
        current_sno: int = 0
        current_category: str = ""

        with csv_path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as file_handle:
            reader = csv.DictReader(file_handle)

            if reader.fieldnames is None:
                raise ValueError("CSV file appears to be empty.")

            # DictReader fieldnames may contain embedded newlines from merged cells.
            normalised_headers = {
                k: re.sub(r"\s+", " ", k).strip() for k in reader.fieldnames
            }

            for row_idx, raw_row in enumerate(reader):
                row = {normalised_headers.get(k, k): v for k, v in raw_row.items()}

                sno_raw = _clean(row.get("Sno", ""))
                cat_raw = _clean(row.get("Category", ""))
                question = _clean(row.get("Question", ""))
                answer = _clean(row.get("Answer", ""))
                rec_type = _clean(row.get("Type", "Main Category"))

                # Rows with neither a question nor an answer carry no information.
                if not question and not answer:
                    LOGGER.debug("Skipping empty row %d", row_idx)
                    continue

                # Forward-fill Sno when the cell is not empty.
                if sno_raw:
                    try:
                        current_sno = int(float(sno_raw))
                    except ValueError:
                        LOGGER.warning(
                            "Non-numeric Sno '%s' at row %d — retaining %d",
                            sno_raw, row_idx, current_sno,
                        )

                # Forward-fill Category when the cell is not empty.
                if cat_raw:
                    current_category = cat_raw

                if not current_category:
                    LOGGER.warning("No category resolved at row %d — skipping", row_idx)
                    continue

                record = QARecord(
                    sno=current_sno,
                    category=current_category,
                    question=question,
                    answer=answer,
                    record_type=rec_type,
                    row_index=row_idx,
                )
                records.append(record)

        LOGGER.info(
            "Loaded %d Q&A records from '%s'.",
            len(records), csv_path.name,
        )
        return records


def get_category_summary(records: list[QARecord]) -> dict[str, int]:
    """
    Return a count of records per category, sorted by count descending.

    Parameters
    ----------
    records : list[QARecord]
        Parsed Q&A records.

    Returns
    -------
    dict[str, int]
        Mapping of category name to record count.
    """
    summary: dict[str, int] = {}
    for rec in records:
        summary[rec.category] = summary.get(rec.category, 0) + 1
    return dict(sorted(summary.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]))



####################################################################################################
FILE: data\es_index_manager.py
####################################################################################################

"""
data/es_index_manager.py
========================
Elasticsearch index lifecycle manager for the YONO Retail knowledge base.

Responsibilities
----------------
- Create the index with the exact mapping from artifect1.txt
  (dense_vector dims=2048, bbq_hnsw, cosine similarity).
- Verify index existence and document count.
- Optionally delete an index with a safety confirmation flag.
- Connection health check with detailed error reporting.

Design
------
Uses the synchronous elasticsearch client for ingestion scripts.
Ingestion is a batch operation, not a web request, so async is not needed.
All credentials come from environment variables and are never hardcoded.
"""

import logging
import os
from typing import Optional

from dotenv import load_dotenv
from elasticsearch import Elasticsearch, NotFoundError
from elasticsearch.exceptions import AuthenticationException, ConnectionError as ESConnectionError

load_dotenv()

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("yb_prelogin_chat")

# Index mapping mirrors artifect1.txt exactly.
# Extra fields (category, question, answer, sno, combined_text, doc_type, etc.)
# are added on top of the base mapping for this dataset.
INDEX_MAPPING: dict = {
    "mappings": {
        "properties": {
            # Core vector fields from artifect1.txt.
            "vector": {
                "type": "dense_vector",
                "dims": 2048,
                "index": True,
                "similarity": "cosine",
                "index_options": {
                    "type": "bbq_hnsw",
                    "m": 16,
                    "ef_construction": 100,
                    "rescore_vector": {
                        "oversample": 3
                    },
                },
            },
            "embedding": {
                "type": "dense_vector",
                "index": True,
                "similarity": "cosine",
            },
            # Primary content fields.
            "content": {"type": "text"},
            "text": {"type": "text"},
            # Q&A specific fields.
            "question": {"type": "text"},
            "answer": {"type": "text"},
            "combined_text": {"type": "text"},
            "category": {
                "type": "text",
                "fields": {"keyword": {"type": "keyword", "ignore_above": 256}},
            },
            "doc_type": {
                "type": "text",
                "fields": {"keyword": {"type": "keyword", "ignore_above": 256}},
            },
            "record_type": {"type": "keyword"},
            # Metadata fields.
            "sno": {"type": "integer"},
            "chunk_size": {"type": "integer"},
            "page_number": {"type": "long"},
            "row_index": {"type": "integer"},
            "source": {"type": "text"},
            "ingested_at": {"type": "text"},
            # RAG-specific keyword fields used by BM25 and LLM metadata.
            "keywords": {
                "type": "text",
                "fields": {"keyword": {"type": "keyword"}},
            },
            "possible_questions": {"type": "text"},
            # Nested metadata block for langchain_postgres compatibility.
            "metadata": {
                "properties": {
                    "source": {
                        "type": "text",
                        "fields": {"keyword": {"type": "keyword", "ignore_above": 256}},
                    },
                    "page_number": {"type": "long"},
                    "chunk_size": {"type": "long"},
                    "ingested_at": {
                        "type": "text",
                        "fields": {"keyword": {"type": "keyword", "ignore_above": 256}},
                    },
                    "category": {"type": "keyword"},
                    "doc_type": {"type": "keyword"},
                }
            },
            "langchain_metadata": {"type": "nested"},
        }
    },
    "settings": {
        "index": {
            "number_of_shards": 1,
            "number_of_replicas": 1,
        }
    },
}


class ElasticsearchIndexManager:
    """
    Manages the Elasticsearch index lifecycle for YONO Retail ingestion.

    Parameters
    ----------
    index_name : str
        Name of the Elasticsearch index.
    es_url : str
        Elasticsearch cluster URL, for example https://host:9200.
    api_key : str
        Elasticsearch API key.
    verify_ssl : bool
        Whether to verify TLS certificates. Set False for self-signed or on-prem.
    request_timeout : int
        HTTP request timeout in seconds.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> manager = ElasticsearchIndexManager.from_env()
    >>> manager.create_index()
    >>> manager.verify_index()
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        index_name: str,
        es_url: str,
        api_key: str,
        verify_ssl: bool = False,
        request_timeout: int = 60,
    ) -> None:
        self._index_name = index_name
        self._client = Elasticsearch(
            es_url,
            api_key=api_key,
            verify_certs=verify_ssl,
            ssl_show_warn=False,
            request_timeout=request_timeout,
            retry_on_timeout=True,
        )

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls) -> "ElasticsearchIndexManager":
        """
        Build an ElasticsearchIndexManager from environment variables.

        Environment variables
        ---------------------
        ES_URL
            Elasticsearch cluster URL.
        ES_API_KEY
            API key for authentication.
        ES_INDEX
            Index name. Defaults to yono_retail_chunks.
        ES_VERIFY_SSL
            Set to true or false. Defaults to false.

        Returns
        -------
        ElasticsearchIndexManager
            Configured instance.
        """
        return cls(
            index_name=os.getenv("ES_INDEX", "yono_retail_chunks"),
            es_url=os.getenv("ES_URL", "https://localhost:9200"),
            api_key=os.getenv("ES_API_KEY", ""),
            verify_ssl=os.getenv("ES_VERIFY_SSL", "false").lower() == "true",
        )

    def health_check(self) -> bool:
        """
        Verify Elasticsearch connectivity and log cluster information.

        Returns
        -------
        bool
            True when the connection is healthy, False otherwise.
        """
        LOGGER.info("Testing connection to Elasticsearch.")
        try:
            info = self._client.info()
            cluster = info.get("cluster_name", "unknown")
            version = info.get("version", {}).get("number", "unknown")
            LOGGER.info(
                "Connection successful. Cluster: %s, Version: %s, Index: %s",
                cluster, version, self._index_name,
            )
            return True
        except AuthenticationException:
            LOGGER.error("Connection failed: authentication error. Check ES_API_KEY.")
        except ESConnectionError as exc:
            if "timeout" in str(exc).lower():
                LOGGER.error("Connection failed: timed out. Check VPN or firewall.")
            else:
                LOGGER.error("Connection failed: %s", exc)
        except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
            LOGGER.error("Connection failed with unexpected error: %s", exc)
        return False

    def index_exists(self) -> bool:
        """
        Check whether the target index already exists.

        Returns
        -------
        bool
            True when the index exists.
        """
        return bool(self._client.indices.exists(index=self._index_name))

    def create_index(self) -> None:
        """
        Create the index with the YONO Retail mapping if it does not already exist.

        The mapping includes the vector field (2048-dim bbq_hnsw cosine)
        from artifect1.txt plus all Q&A metadata fields.
        """
        if self.index_exists():
            LOGGER.info("Index '%s' already exists — skipping creation.", self._index_name)
            return

        LOGGER.info("Creating index '%s'.", self._index_name)
        self._client.indices.create(index=self._index_name, body=INDEX_MAPPING)
        LOGGER.info("Index '%s' created with bbq_hnsw 2048-dim mapping.", self._index_name)

    def verify_index(self) -> int:
        """
        Return the number of documents currently in the index.

        Returns
        -------
        int
            Document count; zero when the index does not exist.
        """
        try:
            result = self._client.count(index=self._index_name)
            count = int(result.get("count", 0))
            LOGGER.info("Index '%s' contains %d documents.", self._index_name, count)
            return count
        except NotFoundError:
            LOGGER.warning("Index '%s' not found during document count.", self._index_name)
            return 0

    def delete_index(self, confirm: bool = False) -> None:
        """
        Delete the index permanently.

        Parameters
        ----------
        confirm : bool
            Must be True to proceed. This is a safety guard against accidental deletion.

        Raises
        ------
        ValueError
            When confirm is False.
        """
        if not confirm:
            raise ValueError("Pass confirm=True to delete the index. This action is irreversible.")
        if not self.index_exists():
            LOGGER.info("Index '%s' does not exist — nothing to delete.", self._index_name)
            return
        self._client.indices.delete(index=self._index_name)
        LOGGER.info("Index '%s' deleted.", self._index_name)

    @property
    def client(self) -> Elasticsearch:
        """Return the underlying Elasticsearch client used by the ingestor."""
        return self._client

    @property
    def index_name(self) -> str:
        """Return the configured index name."""
        return self._index_name


# Backward-compatible alias so existing call sites that import ESIndexManager continue to work.
ESIndexManager = ElasticsearchIndexManager



####################################################################################################
FILE: data\ingest_yono_retail.py
####################################################################################################

"""
data/ingest_yono_retail.py
==========================
End-to-end ingestion pipeline for the YONO Retail Q&A dataset.

What it does
------------
1. Loads dataset.csv via csv_loader.py (handles merged cells).
2. Connects to Elasticsearch via es_index_manager.py.
3. Creates the index with the bbq_hnsw 2048-dim mapping if not present.
4. For each Q&A record:
   a. Embeds the combined_text field using the vLLM embedding endpoint.
   b. Builds a rich Elasticsearch document with all metadata fields.
5. Bulk-indexes documents in configurable batch sizes.
6. Logs a final summary.

Embedding strategy
------------------
The combined_text field is:
    "Category: <category>. Question: <question> Answer: <answer>"

This matches the sample notebook pattern. The embedding model is the external
vLLM endpoint (2048-dim) rather than Elastic's semantic_text.

Usage
-----
Run directly:
    python data/ingest_yono_retail.py

With overrides:
    python data/ingest_yono_retail.py --csv dataset.csv --batch 50 --dry-run

Skip ingestion if index already has data:
    python data/ingest_yono_retail.py --skip-if-exists
"""

import argparse
import logging
import os
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

import httpx
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from elasticsearch import helpers

# Ensure the project root is on the path when this script is run directly.
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))

from data.csv_loader import CsvLoader, QARecord, get_category_summary
from data.es_index_manager import ElasticsearchIndexManager

load_dotenv()

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("yb_prelogin_chat")

# Default values that can be overridden via CLI arguments or environment variables.
DEFAULT_CSV_PATH: Path = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "dataset.csv"
DEFAULT_BATCH_SIZE: int = 50
DEFAULT_EMBED_BATCH: int = 32
DEFAULT_EMBED_TIMEOUT: int = 120


def _build_embedding_client() -> httpx.Client:
    """
    Build a synchronous httpx client for the vLLM embedding endpoint.

    Returns
    -------
    httpx.Client
        Configured HTTP client with SSL verification disabled for on-prem use.
    """
    # SSL verification is disabled because on-prem vLLM endpoints commonly use self-signed certs.
    return httpx.Client(verify=False, timeout=DEFAULT_EMBED_TIMEOUT)


def embed_texts(
    texts: list[str],
    http_client: httpx.Client,
    model_id: str,
    base_url: str,
    api_key: str,
) -> list[list[float]]:
    """
    Embed a batch of texts using the vLLM OpenAI-compatible embedding endpoint.

    Parameters
    ----------
    texts : list[str]
        Input texts to embed.
    http_client : httpx.Client
        Shared HTTP client instance.
    model_id : str
        Embedding model identifier, for example jina-embeddings-v5.
    base_url : str
        Base URL of the vLLM embedding endpoint, for example https://host/v1.
    api_key : str
        API key for the vLLM endpoint.

    Returns
    -------
    list[list[float]]
        List of 2048-dimensional embedding vectors, one per input text.

    Raises
    ------
    RuntimeError
        When the embedding API returns a non-200 HTTP response.
    """
    url = f"{base_url.rstrip('/')}/embeddings"
    payload = {"model": model_id, "input": texts}
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",
    }
    response = http_client.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)

    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Embedding API returned HTTP {response.status_code}: {response.text[:200]}"
        )

    data = response.json().get("data", [])
    # Sort by index to guarantee output order matches input order.
    data_sorted = sorted(data, key=lambda item: item.get("index", 0))
    return [item["embedding"] for item in data_sorted]


def _build_es_document(record: QARecord, vector: list[float]) -> dict:
    """
    Build the Elasticsearch document body for a single Q&A record.

    Parameters
    ----------
    record : QARecord
        Parsed Q&A record.
    vector : list[float]
        2048-dimensional embedding of record.combined_text.

    Returns
    -------
    dict
        Document body matching the index mapping in es_index_manager.py.
    """
    ingested_at = datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc).isoformat()

    # Build a pipe-separated keywords string from category tokens for BM25 retrieval.
    category_tokens = record.category.replace("/", "|").replace("  ", "|")
    keywords = f"{category_tokens}|{record.question[:50]}"

    return {
        # Primary vector field (bbq_hnsw 2048-dim).
        "vector": vector,
        # Content fields.
        "content": record.answer,
        "text": record.answer,
        "combined_text": record.combined_text,
        # Q&A fields.
        "question": record.question,
        "answer": record.answer,
        "category": record.category,
        # doc_type is used by BM25 category_filter.
        "doc_type": record.category,
        "record_type": record.record_type,
        "sno": record.sno,
        "row_index": record.row_index,
        # BM25 retrieval helpers.
        "keywords": keywords,
        # possible_questions is used by BM25 for domain weighting.
        "possible_questions": record.question,
        # Provenance fields.
        "source": "dataset.csv",
        "page_number": record.row_index,
        "chunk_size": len(record.combined_text.split()),
        "ingested_at": ingested_at,
        # Nested metadata block for compatibility with langchain_postgres.
        "metadata": {
            "source": "dataset.csv",
            "page_number": record.row_index,
            "chunk_size": len(record.combined_text.split()),
            "ingested_at": ingested_at,
            "category": record.category,
            "doc_type": record.category,
        },
    }


class IngestionPipeline:
    """
    Orchestrates loading, embedding, and indexing the YONO Retail dataset.

    Parameters
    ----------
    index_manager : ElasticsearchIndexManager
        Configured Elasticsearch index manager.
    batch_size : int
        Number of documents per Elasticsearch bulk request.
    embed_batch_size : int
        Number of texts per embedding API call.
    dry_run : bool
        When True, parse and embed but do not write to Elasticsearch.
    skip_if_exists : bool
        When True and the index already has documents, skip ingestion.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> pipeline = IngestionPipeline.from_env()
    >>> pipeline.run("dataset.csv")
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        index_manager: ElasticsearchIndexManager,
        batch_size: int = DEFAULT_BATCH_SIZE,
        embed_batch_size: int = DEFAULT_EMBED_BATCH,
        dry_run: bool = False,
        skip_if_exists: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        self._mgr = index_manager
        self._batch_size = batch_size
        self._embed_batch = embed_batch_size
        self._dry_run = dry_run
        self._skip_if_exists = skip_if_exists

        # vLLM embedding configuration read from environment variables.
        self._emb_model: str = os.getenv("EMB_MODEL_ID", "")
        self._emb_base_url: str = os.getenv("VLLM_BASE_EMB_URL", "")
        self._emb_api_key: str = os.getenv("VLLM_API_KEY", "")

    @classmethod
    def from_env(
        cls,
        batch_size: int = DEFAULT_BATCH_SIZE,
        embed_batch_size: int = DEFAULT_EMBED_BATCH,
        dry_run: bool = False,
        skip_if_exists: bool = False,
    ) -> "IngestionPipeline":
        """
        Build an IngestionPipeline from environment variables.

        Parameters
        ----------
        batch_size : int
            Number of documents per Elasticsearch bulk request.
        embed_batch_size : int
            Number of texts per embedding API call.
        dry_run : bool
            When True, parse and embed but do not write to Elasticsearch.
        skip_if_exists : bool
            When True and the index already has documents, skip ingestion.

        Returns
        -------
        IngestionPipeline
            Configured pipeline instance.
        """
        return cls(
            index_manager=ElasticsearchIndexManager.from_env(),
            batch_size=batch_size,
            embed_batch_size=embed_batch_size,
            dry_run=dry_run,
            skip_if_exists=skip_if_exists,
        )

    def run(self, csv_path: "str | Path") -> None:
        """
        Execute the full ingestion pipeline.

        Parameters
        ----------
        csv_path : str or Path
            Path to the Q&A CSV file.

        Notes
        -----
        Steps performed:
        1. Health-check Elasticsearch.
        2. Create index if missing.
        3. Skip if already populated and skip_if_exists is set.
        4. Load CSV.
        5. Embed records in batches.
        6. Bulk index to Elasticsearch.
        """
        LOGGER.info("YONO Retail Chatbot — Elasticsearch Ingestion Pipeline starting.")

        if not self._mgr.health_check():
            LOGGER.error("Aborting: cannot reach Elasticsearch.")
            return

        self._mgr.create_index()

        if self._skip_if_exists:
            count = self._mgr.verify_index()
            if count > 0:
                LOGGER.info("Index already contains %d documents. Skipping ingestion.", count)
                return

        LOGGER.info("Loading dataset from: %s", csv_path)
        records = CsvLoader().load(csv_path)
        LOGGER.info("Loaded %d Q&A records.", len(records))

        summary = get_category_summary(records)
        LOGGER.info("Dataset spans %d categories.", len(summary))
        for cat, cnt in list(summary.items())[:15]:
            LOGGER.info("  %4d  %s", cnt, cat)
        if len(summary) > 15:
            LOGGER.info("  ... and %d more categories.", len(summary) - 15)

        if self._dry_run:
            LOGGER.info("Dry run mode — skipping embedding and indexing.")
            return

        self._embed_and_index(records)

    def _embed_and_index(self, records: list[QARecord]) -> None:
        """
        Embed all records in batches and bulk-index into Elasticsearch.

        Parameters
        ----------
        records : list[QARecord]
            All Q&A records to embed and index.
        """
        http_client = _build_embedding_client()
        texts = [r.combined_text for r in records]

        LOGGER.info("Embedding %d texts (batch size=%d).", len(texts), self._embed_batch)
        t0 = time.perf_counter()

        all_vectors: list[list[float]] = []
        for i in range(0, len(texts), self._embed_batch):
            batch_texts = texts[i: i + self._embed_batch]
            batch_num = i // self._embed_batch + 1
            total_batches = (len(texts) + self._embed_batch - 1) // self._embed_batch

            try:
                vectors = embed_texts(
                    batch_texts,
                    http_client=http_client,
                    model_id=self._emb_model,
                    base_url=self._emb_base_url,
                    api_key=self._emb_api_key,
                )
                all_vectors.extend(vectors)
                LOGGER.debug(
                    "Embedding batch %d/%d — %d/%d completed.",
                    batch_num, total_batches, len(all_vectors), len(texts),
                )
            except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
                LOGGER.error("Embedding batch %d failed: %s", batch_num, exc)
                LOGGER.error("Aborting ingestion due to embedding failure.")
                return

        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - t0
        LOGGER.info("Embedding complete: %d vectors in %.1fs.", len(all_vectors), elapsed)

        actions = [
            {
                "_index": self._mgr.index_name,
                "_source": _build_es_document(record, vector),
            }
            for record, vector in zip(records, all_vectors)
        ]

        LOGGER.info("Indexing %d documents (bulk batch size=%d).", len(actions), self._batch_size)
        t1 = time.perf_counter()
        success, failed = helpers.bulk(
            self._mgr.client,
            actions,
            chunk_size=self._batch_size,
            request_timeout=300,
            raise_on_error=False,
        )
        elapsed_idx = time.perf_counter() - t1

        LOGGER.info("Ingestion complete in %.1fs.", elapsed_idx)
        LOGGER.info("Documents indexed: %d.", success)
        if failed:
            LOGGER.warning("Failed documents: %d.", len(failed))
        LOGGER.info("Total documents in index: %d.", self._mgr.verify_index())


def main() -> None:
    """
    Parse CLI arguments and run the ingestion pipeline.

    Notes
    -----
    This function is the CLI entry point. It configures basic logging,
    parses arguments, and delegates to IngestionPipeline.run.
    """
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Ingest YONO Retail Q&A CSV into Elasticsearch",
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
        epilog=(
            "Examples:\n"
            "  python data/ingest_yono_retail.py\n"
            "  python data/ingest_yono_retail.py --csv dataset.csv --batch 100\n"
            "  python data/ingest_yono_retail.py --dry-run\n"
            "  python data/ingest_yono_retail.py --skip-if-exists\n"
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--csv",
        type=Path,
        default=DEFAULT_CSV_PATH,
        help=f"Path to the Q&A CSV file (default: {DEFAULT_CSV_PATH})",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--batch",
        type=int,
        default=DEFAULT_BATCH_SIZE,
        help=f"ES bulk batch size (default: {DEFAULT_BATCH_SIZE})",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--embed-batch",
        type=int,
        default=DEFAULT_EMBED_BATCH,
        help=f"Embedding batch size (default: {DEFAULT_EMBED_BATCH})",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--dry-run",
        action="store_true",
        help="Parse and preview without indexing",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--skip-if-exists",
        action="store_true",
        help="Skip ingestion if the index already has documents",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--health-only",
        action="store_true",
        help="Only test the Elasticsearch connection and exit",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    logging.basicConfig(
        level=logging.INFO,
        format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s",
    )

    if args.health_only:
        mgr = ElasticsearchIndexManager.from_env()
        mgr.health_check()
        mgr.verify_index()
        return

    pipeline = IngestionPipeline.from_env(
        batch_size=args.batch,
        embed_batch_size=args.embed_batch,
        dry_run=args.dry_run,
        skip_if_exists=args.skip_if_exists,
    )
    pipeline.run(args.csv)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



####################################################################################################
