"""
genai/agents/models.py
======================
Shared Pydantic models and TypedDict state for the LangGraph agent pipeline.

All models in this module are immutable data contracts — no business logic lives here.
"""

import logging
from enum import Enum
from typing import Annotated, List, Literal, Optional

from langchain_core.messages import BaseMessage
from langgraph.graph.message import add_messages
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from typing_extensions import TypedDict

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("yb_prelogin_chat")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# LangGraph agent state
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class AgentState(TypedDict):
    """
    Complete mutable state flowing through the LangGraph pipeline.

    Each field is written by exactly one node so that the graph remains
    deterministic and easy to trace during debugging.

    Attributes
    ----------
    messages : list
        LangGraph message bus, accumulated via ``add_messages`` reducer.
    chat_history : str
        Serialised prior turns used to provide conversational context.
    session_id : str
        Unique identifier for the current browser/app session.
    question_id : str
        Unique identifier for a single question-answer pair.
    turn_number : int
        Counter that increments with every user turn in the session.
    intent_analysis : object, optional
        Structured output produced by the unified gate node.
    escalated : bool
        Set to True when the conversation is routed to a live agent.
    query_original : str
        Raw query exactly as received from the user.
    query_normalized : str
        Query after PII removal and whitespace normalisation.
    rewritten_query : str
        Standalone query produced by the rewrite node.
    answer : str
        Final answer string delivered to the user.
    query_tokens : list
        Tokenised form of the query used by the sparse retriever.
    category_filter : str
        Optional product category used to narrow retrieval.
    retrieved_chunks : list
        Raw chunk dictionaries returned by the retriever.
    documents : list
        Ranked ``Document`` objects after reranking.
    top_score : float
        Highest relevance score from the reranker.
    retrieval_attempt : int
        Number of retrieval iterations performed so far.
    status : str
        Pipeline outcome: ``no_results``, ``suggest``, ``answered``,
        ``blocked``, or ``pii_blocked``.
    context : str
        Concatenated chunk text injected into the final-answer prompt.
    relevance : str
        LLM relevance verdict: ``yes``, ``no``, or ``greeting``.
    fallback_used : bool
        True when the response was generated from a fallback path.
    fallback_reason : str
        Human-readable explanation for why the fallback was triggered.
    _t0 : float
        Unix timestamp recorded at pipeline entry for latency tracking.
    """

    # LangGraph message bus — accumulated by the built-in reducer
    messages: Annotated[list, add_messages]
    chat_history: str

    # Session tracking
    session_id: str
    question_id: str
    turn_number: int
    intent_analysis: Optional[object]
    escalated: bool

    # Retrieval pipeline
    query_original: str
    query_normalized: str
    rewritten_query: str
    answer: str
    query_tokens: list
    category_filter: str
    retrieved_chunks: list
    documents: List[object]
    top_score: float
    retrieval_attempt: int
    status: str
    context: str
    relevance: str
    fallback_used: bool
    fallback_reason: str
    _t0: float


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Pydantic structured-output schemas (used with LLM .with_structured_output)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class UrgencyLevel(str, Enum):
    """
    Urgency levels assigned by the intent classification gate.

    Attributes
    ----------
    LOW : str
        Routine informational query with no time pressure.
    MEDIUM : str
        Query that benefits from a prompt response.
    HIGH : str
        Query where delay would cause user frustration.
    CRITICAL : str
        Query involving potential fraud, account block, or safety risk.
    """

    LOW = "low"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    HIGH = "high"
    CRITICAL = "critical"


class QueryClass(str, Enum):
    """
    Top-level LLM gate classifications.

    Attributes
    ----------
    BANKING : str
        Query is within the YONO Retail banking domain.
    EXTRACTION : str
        Query attempts to extract system instructions or training data.
    OFF_TOPIC : str
        Query is outside the supported domain.
    HARMFUL : str
        Query contains content that violates usage policy.
    """

    BANKING = "banking"
    EXTRACTION = "extraction"
    OFF_TOPIC = "off_topic"
    HARMFUL = "harmful"


class UnifiedGateResult(BaseModel):
    """
    Structured output from the unified security and intent gate.

    This schema is bound to the gate LLM via ``with_structured_output``
    so that every field is guaranteed to be present and typed.

    Attributes
    ----------
    classification : QueryClass
        Top-level routing decision for the incoming query.
    intent : str
        Primary user intent label (e.g. ``account_balance``).
    urgency : UrgencyLevel
        Urgency level inferred from query tone and content.
    escalate_to_agent : bool
        True when the pipeline should hand off to a live agent.
    escalation_reason : str, optional
        Plain-English explanation of why escalation was triggered.
    sentiment : str
        User sentiment inferred from the query (e.g. ``neutral``, ``angry``).
    """

    classification: QueryClass = Field(description="Top-level query classification.")
    intent: str = Field(default="general_info", description="Primary user intent.")
    urgency: UrgencyLevel = Field(default=UrgencyLevel.LOW, description="Urgency level.")
    escalate_to_agent: bool = Field(default=False, description="Whether to escalate to a live agent.")
    escalation_reason: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, description="Reason for escalation.")
    sentiment: str = Field(default="neutral", description="User sentiment.")


class QueryRewriteResponse(BaseModel):
    """
    Structured output from the query rewrite node.

    Attributes
    ----------
    is_related : bool
        True when the current query depends on prior conversation context.
    rewritten_query : str
        Final standalone query with all coreferences resolved.
    """

    is_related: bool = Field(description="Whether the current query depends on the previous context.")
    rewritten_query: str = Field(description="Final standalone rewritten query.")


class YonoRelevance(BaseModel):
    """
    Relevance gate output retained for backward compatibility.

    Attributes
    ----------
    is_related : Literal["yes", "no", "greeting"]
        Whether the question is related to YONO Retail products.
        The value ``greeting`` is used for salutations that require no retrieval.
    """

    is_related: Literal["yes", "no", "greeting"] = Field(
        description="Whether the question is related to YONO Retail products."
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# FastAPI request / response models
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    """
    Unencrypted chat request body accepted by the chat endpoint.

    Attributes
    ----------
    question : str
        User-supplied question text.
    thread_id : str
        Client-managed conversation thread identifier.
    """

    question: str
    thread_id: str


class FeedbackRequest(BaseModel):
    """
    Unencrypted feedback request body accepted by the feedback endpoint.

    Attributes
    ----------
    question_id : str
        Identifier of the question-answer pair being rated.
    feedback : str
        User feedback value (e.g. ``thumbs_up``, ``thumbs_down``).
    """

    question_id: str
    feedback: str


class EscalationStatusUpdate(BaseModel):
    """
    Escalation status update posted from the agent dashboard.

    Attributes
    ----------
    escalation_id : str
        Unique identifier of the escalation record.
    status : str
        New status value: ``pending``, ``accepted``, or ``resolved``.
    agent_id : str
        Identifier of the human agent processing the escalation.
    """

    escalation_id: str
    # Valid transitions: pending -> accepted -> resolved
    status: str
    agent_id: str



####################################################################################################
FILE: genai\agents\nodes.py
####################################################################################################

"""
genai/agents/nodes.py
=====================
Individual LangGraph node functions for the YONO Retail agentic RAG pipeline.

Each node is a pure async function that receives an AgentState dict and returns
a (partial) AgentState dict with updated fields.

Node execution order
--------------------
START
  -> preflight_node              (input abuse, PII, greeting, gibberish)
  -> security_and_intent_node   (language gate, regex guard, LLM gate)
  -> query_analysis_node        (normalise, expand, rewrite with context)
  -> retrieve_chunks_node       (hybrid dense+sparse ES retrieval)
  -> rerank_chunks_node         (cross-encoder reranker)
  -> generate_final_response_node (LLM generation)
  -> finalise_node              (disclaimer, strip hidden tags, persist)
END
"""

import logging
import re
import time
import unicodedata
from typing import Optional

from langchain_core.messages import AIMessage, HumanMessage

from genai.agents.models import (
    AgentState,
    QueryClass,
    QueryRewriteResponse,
    UnifiedGateResult,
    UrgencyLevel,
)
from genai.core.conversation_db import ConversationDB
from genai.prompt._manager import PromptManager
from genai.rag.retriever import HybridRetriever
from genai.rag.reranker import Reranker
from utils.preprocessor import build_language_response, preprocess_query
from utils.security import (
    GREETINGS,
    THANK_YOU,
    UnifiedLLMGate,
    check_input_abuse,
    check_pii,
    is_gibberish,
    normalise_unicode,
    unified_regex_guard,
    validate_and_clean_input,
)

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("yb_prelogin_chat")

# Process-lifetime singletons are created once per worker to avoid repeated
# initialisation overhead on every request.
_db = ConversationDB()
_retriever = HybridRetriever(top_k=5, dense_pool=20, sparse_pool=30)
_reranker = Reranker(top_n=5)
_prompt_mgr = PromptManager.instance()

# LLM client is injected at pipeline build time via functools.partial in pipeline.py.

# Patterns that indicate a prompt-injection attempt embedded in retrieved context.
_CONTEXT_INJECTION_PATTERNS: list[re.Pattern] = [
    re.compile(r"ignore\s+(all\s+)?previous\s+instructions?", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"new\s+instruction[s:]", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"\[INST\].*?\[/INST\]", re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL),
    re.compile(r"<\s*system\s*>.*?<\s*/\s*system\s*>", re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL),
    re.compile(r"you\s+are\s+now\s+(a|an)", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"disregard\s+(all\s+)?(previous|prior)\s+(instructions?|rules?)", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"reveal\s+(your\s+)?(system\s+)?prompt", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"print\s+everything\s+above", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"access\s+granted", re.IGNORECASE),
]

# Compiled once at module load to avoid recompilation on every preflight call.
_SPECIAL_ONLY_PATTERN: re.Pattern = re.compile(r"^[\s\W_\d]+$")


class _NodeHelpers:
    """
    Private collection of stateless helper utilities shared across node functions.

    This class is not intended to be instantiated; all methods are static.
    Grouping helpers here avoids polluting module scope with loose functions
    while keeping them accessible to every node in this module.
    """

    @staticmethod
    def sanitise_context(context: str) -> str:
        """
        Remove lines matching prompt-injection patterns from retrieved context.

        Injected instructions inside document chunks could redirect the LLM,
        so we strip any matching lines before they reach the generation prompt.

        Parameters
        ----------
        context : str
            Raw context string assembled from retrieved chunks.

        Returns
        -------
        str
            Sanitised context with injection lines replaced by a placeholder.
        """
        if not context:
            return context

        lines = context.splitlines()
        cleaned: list[str] = []
        removed = 0

        for line in lines:
            if any(pattern.search(line) for pattern in _CONTEXT_INJECTION_PATTERNS):
                removed += 1
                cleaned.append("[content removed]")
            else:
                cleaned.append(line)

        if removed:
            LOGGER.warning("sanitise_context: removed %d injection line(s).", removed)

        return "\n".join(cleaned)

    @staticmethod
    def normalise_query(query: str) -> dict:
        """
        Normalise a raw user query using Unicode NFKC, lowercase, and whitespace collapse.

        Parameters
        ----------
        query : str
            Raw user input string.

        Returns
        -------
        dict
            Dictionary with keys ``normalized_text`` and ``original``.
        """
        original = query.strip()
        text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", original)
        text = re.sub(r"[^\w\s\-]", " ", text.lower())
        text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()
        return {"normalized_text": text, "original": original}

    @staticmethod
    async def persist_blocked(
        question_id: str,
        session_id: str,
        turn_number: int,
        question: str,
        answer: str,
        is_relevant: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Persist a blocked or short-circuit response to the conversation store.

        Centralising persistence logic here prevents duplicated save_message
        calls scattered across every branch of the preflight and security nodes.

        Parameters
        ----------
        question_id : str
            Unique identifier for this question.
        session_id : str
            Session or thread identifier.
        turn_number : int
            Conversation turn index (1-based).
        question : str
            Original user question text.
        answer : str
            Response text sent back to the user.
        is_relevant : bool, optional
            Whether the response is considered on-topic. Defaults to False.
        """
        await _db.save_message(
            question_id=question_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            turn_number=turn_number,
            question=question,
            answer=answer,
            is_relevent=is_relevant,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def blocked_state(
        state: AgentState,
        answer: str,
        status: str = "blocked",
    ) -> AgentState:
        """
        Build a state update dict that short-circuits the pipeline.

        Using a helper here keeps the return sites in each node concise and
        consistent, reducing the risk of omitting a required field.

        Parameters
        ----------
        state : AgentState
            Current pipeline state to merge into.
        answer : str
            Response text to deliver to the user.
        status : str, optional
            Status code for the pipeline. Defaults to ``"blocked"``.

        Returns
        -------
        AgentState
            Merged state with pipeline fields set to their terminal values.
        """
        return {
            **state,
            "answer": answer,
            "status": status,
            "relevance": "no",
            "escalated": False,
            "intent_analysis": None,
        }


# ---- Node: preflight -----------------------------------------------------------

async def preflight_node(state: AgentState) -> AgentState:
    """
    Gate 1 - input abuse, PII, gibberish, special characters, greetings.

    This node is the first line of defence. It rejects requests that are
    structurally invalid, abusive, PII-laden, or trivially short-circuited
    (greetings and thank-you messages) before any LLM call is made.

    Parameters
    ----------
    state : AgentState
        Current pipeline state containing the incoming user message.

    Returns
    -------
    AgentState
        Updated state. ``status`` is set to ``"blocked"`` and ``relevance``
        to ``"no"`` if the input is rejected; otherwise ``status`` is ``"ok"``.
    """
    raw_question: str = state["messages"][-1].content
    session_id: str = state["session_id"]
    turn_number: int = state["turn_number"]
    question_id: str = state["question_id"]

    LOGGER.info("preflight_node - session=%s", session_id)

    # Full validation catches injection attempts, excessive length, and repetition.
    is_safe, _cleaned, rejection_reason = validate_and_clean_input(raw_question)
    if not is_safe:
        LOGGER.warning("preflight: blocked - %s", rejection_reason)
        await _NodeHelpers.persist_blocked(
            question_id=question_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            turn_number=turn_number + 1,
            question=raw_question,
            answer=rejection_reason,
        )
        return _NodeHelpers.blocked_state(state, rejection_reason)

    # Unicode normalisation prevents homoglyph bypass of downstream checks.
    raw_question = normalise_unicode(raw_question)

    is_abusive, abuse_msg = check_input_abuse(raw_question)
    if is_abusive:
        await _NodeHelpers.persist_blocked(
            question_id=question_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            turn_number=turn_number + 1,
            question=raw_question[:200],
            answer=abuse_msg,
        )
        return _NodeHelpers.blocked_state(state, abuse_msg)

    pii_found, pii_msg = check_pii(raw_question)
    if pii_found:
        return _NodeHelpers.blocked_state(state, pii_msg, status="pii_blocked")

    # Reject queries that contain only punctuation, digits, or whitespace.
    if _SPECIAL_ONLY_PATTERN.match(raw_question):
        block_msg = "To assist you better, please enter meaningful text."
        await _NodeHelpers.persist_blocked(
            question_id=question_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            turn_number=turn_number + 1,
            question=raw_question,
            answer=block_msg,
        )
        return _NodeHelpers.blocked_state(state, block_msg)

    # Short-circuit greetings with a canned response to avoid wasting LLM calls.
    if GREETINGS.match(raw_question):
        greet_msg = "Hello, how can I help you today?"
        await _NodeHelpers.persist_blocked(
            question_id=question_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            turn_number=turn_number + 1,
            question=raw_question,
            answer=greet_msg,
        )
        return _NodeHelpers.blocked_state(state, greet_msg)

    if THANK_YOU.match(raw_question):
        thank_msg = "You are welcome. Feel free to reach out if you need any further assistance."
        await _NodeHelpers.persist_blocked(
            question_id=question_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            turn_number=turn_number + 1,
            question=raw_question,
            answer=thank_msg,
        )
        return _NodeHelpers.blocked_state(state, thank_msg)

    if is_gibberish(raw_question):
        block_msg = "To assist you better, please enter meaningful text."
        await _NodeHelpers.persist_blocked(
            question_id=question_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            turn_number=turn_number + 1,
            question=raw_question,
            answer=block_msg,
        )
        return _NodeHelpers.blocked_state(state, block_msg)

    return {**state, "status": "ok"}


# ---- Node: security and intent -------------------------------------------------

async def security_and_intent_node(
    state: AgentState,
    llm_gate: UnifiedLLMGate,
) -> AgentState:
    """
    Gate 2 - language detection, regex guard, LLM classification, intent analysis.

    Three sequential checks protect against non-English input, pattern-matched
    harmful queries, and out-of-domain questions before retrieval begins.

    Parameters
    ----------
    state : AgentState
        Current pipeline state.
    llm_gate : UnifiedLLMGate
        Injected LLM gate instance responsible for classification and intent.

    Returns
    -------
    AgentState
        Updated state. ``escalated`` or ``relevance="no"`` signals a block.
    """
    raw_question: str = state["messages"][-1].content
    session_id: str = state["session_id"]
    turn_number: int = state["turn_number"]
    question_id: str = state["question_id"]

    LOGGER.info("security_and_intent_node - session=%s", session_id)

    # Non-English queries cannot be answered accurately from the English knowledge base.
    pre = preprocess_query(raw_question)
    if pre.language_blocked:
        lang_msg = build_language_response(pre.language)
        await _db.save_message(
            question_id=question_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            question=raw_question,
            answer=lang_msg,
            turn_number=turn_number + 1,
            escalated=False,
            is_relevent=False,
        )
        return {
            **state,
            "answer": lang_msg,
            "relevance": "no",
            "escalated": False,
            "intent_analysis": None,
        }

    cleaned_pre: str = pre.processed

    # Pattern-based guard runs before the LLM to catch obviously harmful queries cheaply.
    is_blocked, cleaned, rejection_msg = unified_regex_guard(cleaned_pre)
    if is_blocked:
        await _db.save_message(
            question_id=question_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            question=raw_question,
            answer=rejection_msg,
            turn_number=turn_number + 1,
            escalated=False,
            is_relevent=False,
        )
        return {
            **state,
            "answer": rejection_msg,
            "relevance": "no",
            "escalated": False,
            "intent_analysis": None,
        }

    # LLM gate classifies the query domain and extracts intent metadata.
    result: UnifiedGateResult = await llm_gate.evaluate(
        query=cleaned,
        conversation_history=state["chat_history"],
        turn_number=state["turn_number"],
    )

    if result.classification != QueryClass.BANKING:
        msg = llm_gate.rejection_message(result)
        await _db.save_message(
            question_id=question_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            question=raw_question,
            answer=msg,
            turn_number=turn_number + 1,
            escalated=False,
        )
        return {
            **state,
            "answer": msg,
            "relevance": "no",
            "escalated": False,
            "intent_analysis": result,
        }

    return {
        **state,
        "messages": state["messages"][:-1] + [HumanMessage(content=cleaned)],
        "escalated": False,
        "intent_analysis": None,
    }


# ---- Node: query analysis ------------------------------------------------------

async def query_analysis_node(state: AgentState, llm_client) -> AgentState:
    """
    Normalise the query, expand acronyms, and rewrite using conversation context.

    Query rewriting improves retrieval recall by resolving pronouns and
    co-references that are implicit when the user continues a prior topic.

    Parameters
    ----------
    state : AgentState
        Current pipeline state.
    llm_client : ChatOpenAI
        Injected LLM client used for structured query rewriting.

    Returns
    -------
    AgentState
        Updated state with ``query_normalized`` and ``rewritten_query`` fields set.
    """
    LOGGER.info("query_analysis_node")

    raw: str = state["messages"][-1].content
    session_id: str = state["session_id"]
    chat_history: str = state["chat_history"]

    nq: dict = _NodeHelpers.normalise_query(raw)
    current_question: str = nq["normalized_text"]
    LOGGER.info("Normalised query: %s", current_question)

    last_query: Optional[str] = await _db.get_last_query(session_id)
    LOGGER.info("Last query: %s", last_query)

    # Default to the normalised form so retrieval still works if rewrite fails.
    rewritten_query: str = current_question
    try:
        structured_llm = llm_client.with_structured_output(QueryRewriteResponse)
        system_prompt = _prompt_mgr.get("query_rewrite_system")
        user_prompt = (
            f"Previous Query:\n{last_query}\n\n"
            f"Conversation history:\n{chat_history}\n\n"
            f"Current Query:\n{current_question}"
        )
        result: QueryRewriteResponse = structured_llm.invoke([
            {"role": "system", "content": system_prompt},
            {"role": "user", "content": user_prompt},
        ])
        rewritten_query = result.rewritten_query
        LOGGER.info("Rewritten query: %s", rewritten_query)
    except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
        LOGGER.error("Query rewrite error: %s - using normalised query.", exc)

    return {
        **state,
        "query_original": raw,
        "query_normalized": current_question,
        "rewritten_query": rewritten_query,
        "retrieval_attempt": 0,
        "status": "no_results",
        "fallback_used": False,
        "fallback_reason": "",
        "_t0": time.perf_counter(),
    }


# ---- Node: retrieve chunks -----------------------------------------------------

async def retrieve_chunks_node(state: AgentState) -> AgentState:
    """
    Run the hybrid dense+sparse Elasticsearch retriever.

    Hybrid retrieval combines dense semantic search with sparse BM25 keyword
    matching to improve recall across both semantic and exact-match queries.

    Parameters
    ----------
    state : AgentState
        Current pipeline state.

    Returns
    -------
    AgentState
        Updated state with ``documents`` populated from the retrieval results.
    """
    LOGGER.info("retrieve_chunks_node")
    try:
        rewritten_query: str = state["rewritten_query"]
        category_filter: str = state.get("category_filter", "")
        docs = await _retriever.retrieve(rewritten_query, category_filter=category_filter)
        # Reciprocal rank fusion score for the top document at rank 1.
        top_score: float = 1.0 / (60 + 1) if docs else 0.0
        return {
            **state,
            "documents": docs,
            "retrieved_chunks": [],
            "top_score": top_score,
            "retrieval_attempt": state["retrieval_attempt"] + 1,
        }
    except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
        LOGGER.error("retrieve_chunks_node error: %s", exc)
        return {**state, "documents": [], "top_score": 0.0}


# ---- Node: rerank chunks -------------------------------------------------------

async def rerank_chunks_node(state: AgentState) -> AgentState:
    """
    Re-rank the retrieved documents using the cross-encoder reranker.

    Cross-encoder reranking provides a more accurate relevance signal than
    the initial retrieval scores, ensuring only on-topic chunks reach the LLM.

    Parameters
    ----------
    state : AgentState
        Current pipeline state.

    Returns
    -------
    AgentState
        Updated state with ``context`` assembled from top-ranked chunks and
        ``relevance`` set to ``"yes"`` or ``"no"``.
    """
    LOGGER.info("rerank_chunks_node")
    docs = state.get("documents", [])

    if not docs:
        return {
            **state,
            "context": "",
            "relevance": "no",
            "answer": (
                "I am here to provide information on retail banking products. "
                "Please ask me about YONO, accounts, loans, or other SBI services."
            ),
        }

    no_info_msg = (
        "I am here to provide information on YONO Retail banking products. "
        "Your query seems to be on a different topic. "
        "Please ask me about SBI accounts, loans, cards, payments, or other YONO services."
    )

    try:
        context, is_relevant = await _reranker.rerank(state["rewritten_query"], docs)
        if not is_relevant:
            await _db.save_message(
                question_id=state["question_id"],
                session_id=state["session_id"],
                turn_number=state["turn_number"] + 1,
                is_relevent=False,
                question=state["messages"][-1].content,
                answer=no_info_msg,
            )
            return {**state, "relevance": "no", "answer": no_info_msg, "context": ""}

        return {**state, "context": context, "relevance": "yes"}

    except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
        LOGGER.error("rerank_chunks_node error: %s", exc)
        # Best-effort fallback so the pipeline can still attempt generation.
        fallback_context = "\n\n".join(d.page_content for d in docs[:5])
        return {**state, "context": fallback_context, "relevance": "yes"}


# ---- Node: generate final response ---------------------------------------------

async def generate_final_response_node(state: AgentState, llm_client) -> AgentState:
    """
    Generate the final LLM response using the retrieved and reranked context.

    The context is sanitised before injection to prevent prompt-injection
    attacks that may have survived the earlier document-level checks.

    Parameters
    ----------
    state : AgentState
        Current pipeline state.
    llm_client : ChatOpenAI
        Injected LLM client used for response generation.

    Returns
    -------
    AgentState
        Updated state with ``answer`` populated and ``status`` set to
        ``"answered"``.
    """
    LOGGER.info("generate_final_response_node")

    question: str = state["messages"][-1].content
    rewritten_query: str = state["rewritten_query"]
    context: str = _NodeHelpers.sanitise_context(state.get("context", ""))
    session_id: str = state["session_id"]
    question_id: str = state["question_id"]

    system_prompt: str = _prompt_mgr.get("final_response_system")
    full_prompt: str = (
        f"{system_prompt}\n\n"
        f"[CONTEXT]\n{context or 'No relevant information found.'}\n\n"
        f"[CUSTOMER QUESTION]\n{rewritten_query}\n\n"
        f"Now write your response:"
    )

    try:
        ai_response = llm_client.invoke(full_prompt)
        response_text: str = ai_response.content
    except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-exception-caught
        LOGGER.error("LLM generation error: %s", exc)
        response_text = (
            "I'm having trouble retrieving that information right now. "
            "Please try again or contact our helpline at 1800-11-2211."
        )

    LOGGER.info("Response preview: %s", response_text[:120])

    ia = state.get("intent_analysis")
    await _db.save_message(
        question_id=question_id,
        session_id=session_id,
        turn_number=state["turn_number"] + 1,
        question=question,
        answer=response_text,
        rewritten_query=rewritten_query,
        urgency=ia.urgency.value if ia else None,
        sentiment=ia.sentiment if ia else None,
        is_relevent=True,
    )

    return {**state, "status": "answered", "answer": response_text}


# ---- Node: finalise ------------------------------------------------------------

async def finalise_node(state: AgentState) -> AgentState:
    """
    Finalise the pipeline - strip hidden tags and prepend disclaimer on turn 1.

    Stripping [category:...] tags prevents internal routing metadata from
    leaking into the user-facing response text.

    Parameters
    ----------
    state : AgentState
        Current pipeline state.

    Returns
    -------
    AgentState
        Updated state ready to be returned to the calling application layer.
    """
    latency: float = round(
        (time.perf_counter() - state.get("_t0", time.perf_counter())) * 1000, 1
    )
    LOGGER.info(
        "finalise | status=%s | %.0fms | fallback=%s",
        state.get("status"),
        latency,
        state.get("fallback_used"),
    )

    answer: str = state["answer"]
    # Strip internal routing tags injected during generation before delivery.
    answer = re.sub(r"\[category:[^\]]*\]", "", answer).strip()

    # The disclaimer is prepended only on turn 1 to avoid repeating it every reply.
    if state["turn_number"] == 1:
        disclaimer: str = _prompt_mgr.get("disclaimer")
        answer = f"{disclaimer}\n\n{answer}"

    return {
        **state,
        "answer": answer,
        "messages": state["messages"] + [AIMessage(content=answer)],
    }



####################################################################################################
FILE: genai\agents\pipeline.py
####################################################################################################

"""
genai/agents/pipeline.py
========================
LangGraph pipeline assembly and public entry point for the YONO Retail Chatbot.

This module encapsulates all pipeline concerns inside AgentPipeline:
  1. Instantiates the LLM and gate clients (once per process via singleton).
  2. Wires the agent nodes into a LangGraph StateGraph.
  3. Compiles the graph.
  4. Exposes an async run() method consumed by the thin module-level wrapper.

The module-level agent_backend() function is the single entry point called
by the FastAPI application layer. It delegates entirely to the singleton
AgentPipeline instance, keeping the public surface area minimal.

Design
------
All node functions are imported from nodes.py and wrapped with
functools.partial to inject LLM client dependencies without introducing
global mutable state in the node module itself.

The compiled graph uses LangGraph's async ainvoke API, which is safe under
FastAPI's async event loop.
"""

import logging
import time
import uuid
from functools import partial
from typing import Optional

import httpx
from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage
from langchain_openai import ChatOpenAI
from langgraph.graph import END, START, StateGraph

from config import get_llm_config
from genai.agents.models import AgentState
from genai.agents.nodes import (
    finalise_node,
    generate_final_response_node,
    preflight_node,
    query_analysis_node,
    rerank_chunks_node,
    retrieve_chunks_node,
    security_and_intent_node,
)
from genai.core.conversation_db import ConversationDB
from utils.security import UnifiedLLMGate

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("yb_prelogin_chat")


class AgentPipeline:
    """
    Encapsulates LLM client construction, graph wiring, and query execution.

    A single instance is created at module load time and reused for every
    request. This avoids rebuilding the graph and LLM client on each call,
    which would be expensive and could cause resource leaks.

    Attributes
    ----------
    _llm_client : ChatOpenAI
        Shared LLM client used by query analysis and generation nodes.
    _unified_gate : UnifiedLLMGate
        Security and intent classification gate wrapping the LLM client.
    _db : ConversationDB
        Conversation persistence layer used to fetch turn metadata.
    _graph : object
        Compiled LangGraph state machine ready for async invocation.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._llm_client: ChatOpenAI = self._build_llm_client()
        self._unified_gate: UnifiedLLMGate = UnifiedLLMGate(self._llm_client)
        self._db: ConversationDB = ConversationDB()
        self._graph = self._build_graph()

    def _build_llm_client(self) -> ChatOpenAI:
        """
        Construct the shared on-prem LLM client from application configuration.

        The client targets an OpenAI-compatible HTTP endpoint (e.g. DLH gateway,
        vLLM).  SSL verification is controlled by the ``LLM_VERIFY_SSL`` setting,
        which defaults to ``False`` for internal deployments that use self-signed
        certificates.

        Returns
        -------
        ChatOpenAI
            Configured LangChain ChatOpenAI client pointing at the on-prem endpoint.
        """
        cfg = get_llm_config()
        LOGGER.info(
            "Building LLM client - model=%s base_url=%s verify_ssl=%s",
            cfg.model_id,
            cfg.base_url,
            cfg.verify_ssl,
        )
        # Pass verify=False only when the operator has opted out of TLS verification.
        ssl_verify: bool = cfg.verify_ssl
        return ChatOpenAI(
            model=cfg.model_id,
            openai_api_key=cfg.api_key,
            base_url=cfg.base_url,
            http_async_client=httpx.AsyncClient(verify=ssl_verify, timeout=cfg.timeout_seconds),
            http_client=httpx.Client(verify=ssl_verify, timeout=cfg.timeout_seconds),
            max_tokens=cfg.max_tokens,
            temperature=cfg.temperature,
        )

    def _build_graph(self) -> object:
        """
        Construct, configure, and compile the LangGraph state machine.

        LLM-dependent nodes receive their client via functools.partial so that
        the node functions themselves remain free of global state. This makes
        them straightforward to unit-test with a mock client.

        Returns
        -------
        object
            Compiled LangGraph runnable ready for ainvoke calls.
        """
        graph: StateGraph = StateGraph(AgentState)

        # Register nodes with injected dependencies bound via partial.
        graph.add_node("preflight_node", preflight_node)
        graph.add_node(
            "security_and_intent",
            partial(security_and_intent_node, llm_gate=self._unified_gate),
        )
        graph.add_node(
            "query_analysis",
            partial(query_analysis_node, llm_client=self._llm_client),
        )
        graph.add_node("retrieve_chunks", retrieve_chunks_node)
        graph.add_node("rerank_chunks", rerank_chunks_node)
        graph.add_node(
            "generate_final_response",
            partial(generate_final_response_node, llm_client=self._llm_client),
        )
        graph.add_node("finalise", finalise_node)

        graph.add_edge(START, "preflight_node")

        graph.add_conditional_edges(
            "preflight_node",
            self._route_after_preflight,
            {"intent_node": "security_and_intent", "END": "finalise"},
        )
        graph.add_conditional_edges(
            "security_and_intent",
            self._route_after_security,
            {"query_analysis": "query_analysis", "END": "finalise"},
        )
        graph.add_edge("query_analysis", "retrieve_chunks")
        graph.add_edge("retrieve_chunks", "rerank_chunks")
        graph.add_conditional_edges(
            "rerank_chunks",
            self._route_after_rerank,
            {"generate_final_response": "generate_final_response", "END": "finalise"},
        )
        graph.add_edge("generate_final_response", "finalise")
        graph.add_edge("finalise", END)

        compiled = graph.compile()
        LOGGER.info("LangGraph pipeline compiled successfully.")
        return compiled

    @staticmethod
    def _route_after_preflight(state: AgentState) -> str:
        """
        Route the graph after the preflight node completes.

        Parameters
        ----------
        state : AgentState
            Current pipeline state produced by preflight_node.

        Returns
        -------
        str
            ``"END"`` if the input was blocked; otherwise ``"intent_node"``.
        """
        if state.get("status") != "ok":
            return "END"
        return "intent_node"

    @staticmethod
    def _route_after_security(state: AgentState) -> str:
        """
        Route the graph after the security and intent node completes.

        Parameters
        ----------
        state : AgentState
            Current pipeline state produced by security_and_intent_node.

        Returns
        -------
        str
            ``"END"`` if the query was blocked or escalated; otherwise
            ``"query_analysis"``.
        """
        if state.get("escalated") or state.get("relevance") in ("escalated", "no"):
            return "END"
        return "query_analysis"

    @staticmethod
    def _route_after_rerank(state: AgentState) -> str:
        """
        Route the graph after the rerank node completes.

        Parameters
        ----------
        state : AgentState
            Current pipeline state produced by rerank_chunks_node.

        Returns
        -------
        str
            ``"END"`` if no relevant chunks were found; otherwise
            ``"generate_final_response"``.
        """
        if state.get("relevance") == "no":
            return "END"
        return "generate_final_response"

    async def run(self, query: str, thread_id: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
        """
        Execute the full pipeline for one user query and return the response.

        Parameters
        ----------
        query : str
            Raw user question received from the application layer.
        thread_id : str
            Session or thread identifier supplied by the client.

        Returns
        -------
        tuple[str, str]
            A two-element tuple ``(response_text, question_id)``.
            ``question_id`` is a fresh UUID generated per question and
            is used downstream for feedback linking.

        Notes
        -----
        Conversation persistence is handled inside the individual nodes.
        This method is responsible only for assembling the initial state
        and extracting the final response from the graph output.
        """
        query = query.lower()
        LOGGER.info("[session=%s] Question: %s", thread_id, query)

        turn_number: int = await self._db.get_next_turn_number(thread_id)
        chat_history: str = await self._db.format_history_for_prompt(thread_id, limit=2)
        question_id: str = str(uuid.uuid4())

        inputs: AgentState = {
            "messages": [HumanMessage(content=query)],
            "chat_history": chat_history,
            "session_id": thread_id,
            "question_id": question_id,
            "turn_number": turn_number,
            "intent_analysis": None,
            "escalated": False,
            "query_original": query,
            "query_normalized": "",
            "query_tokens": [],
            "answer": "",
            "rewritten_query": "",
            "category_filter": "",
            "retrieved_chunks": [],
            "documents": [],
            "top_score": 0.0,
            "retrieval_attempt": 0,
            "status": "no_results",
            "context": "",
            "relevance": "",
            "fallback_used": False,
            "fallback_reason": "",
            "_t0": time.perf_counter(),
        }

        response = await self._graph.ainvoke(inputs)
        agent_response: str = response["messages"][-1].content

        LOGGER.info(
            "[session=%s] [qid=%s] Response: %s",
            thread_id,
            question_id,
            agent_response[:120],
        )
        return agent_response, question_id


# Module-level singleton initialised once when the module is first imported.
# This matches the lifecycle of a FastAPI worker process.
_pipeline: Optional[AgentPipeline] = None


def _get_pipeline() -> AgentPipeline:
    """
    Return the module-level AgentPipeline singleton, creating it if necessary.

    Using a lazy initialisation function rather than a bare module-level
    assignment makes it easier to reset the singleton in tests.

    Returns
    -------
    AgentPipeline
        The shared pipeline instance for this process.
    """
    global _pipeline  # pylint: disable=global-statement
    if _pipeline is None:
        _pipeline = AgentPipeline()
    return _pipeline


async def agent_backend(query: str, thread_id: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """
    Main entry point called by the FastAPI application layer.

    This function is a thin wrapper that delegates to the AgentPipeline
    singleton. Keeping the public API as a module-level function avoids
    callers needing to know about the AgentPipeline class directly.

    Parameters
    ----------
    query : str
        Raw user question received from the HTTP request.
    thread_id : str
        Session or thread identifier from the client.

    Returns
    -------
    tuple[str, str]
        ``(response_text, question_id)``

    Notes
    -----
    question_id is a new UUID generated per question for feedback linking.
    All conversation persistence is handled inside the individual nodes.
    """
    return await _get_pipeline().run(query=query, thread_id=thread_id)
